# About

The planet on the list page: a sphere of 20480 triangles, described in the Topology section below.

A triangle is pinpointed by **(ring, index)**:

- **ring**: 0–95, counted from the north pole southwards. Ring N is the section `## Ring N`.
- **index**: the triangle's place along its ring, counted eastwards from 0. Index I is the
  (I + 1)-th colour line of the ring's section.

Every ring section starts with a `Climate: <climate zone>` line: polar, sub-polar, temperate,
sub-tropical or tropical. A triangle line holds, space-separated: the triangle's colour, `L` if the
triangle is land, `F` if the land is forest or `D` if it is desert, and its biome: polar, temperate
or tropical.
